Opening an entity picker with no filter on previews an empty pick (first click commits only that entity). The People picker ends with a footer (`PeopleReviewAction`, `.reviewHint` / `.reviewAction` in `ScreenFilters.module.css`; only while `useEntityMenuOffers().review` — outside a dialog the task dialog can't show above): while the filter is narrowed to some people (`narrowedPeople` in `FilterChipBar.shared.ts` — not everyone, not only None / nobody) the Review item (`person-menu-entries.peopleMenuEntries`, D26) as a button — one person's `Review with <name>`, several people's `Review N people’s tasks` —, a jump to a Review widget with them (`@A or @B`); it closes the picker (and the Filter menu); otherwise the muted hint `Narrow to some people to review them.`. No check-in items (they live on the Review widget's field). **Mobile**: the accordion bottom-sheet shape in the same order — Priority (the Status list's rows and Is / Is not over the markers and `No priority`, no search; with an `is not` the row shows the value (`not Urgent`) in the accent instead of a count); Show's `Any` means has the date —; the body is `DateFilterPanel variant="sheet"`), Status — then Show / hide (Completed); a People / Tags / Status row is a label round a real checkbox (Tab, Space, a screen reader). The Filter menu's Dates submenu is `DateFilterPanel` in a `Date filters` dialog (`date-filter-popover`: 336px, no padding). A People / Tags / Status checklist (desktop `EntityDropdownItems`, phone `MobileEntityList` — Tags too) lists the ticked rows first (alphabetical; the None row first of its block), a separator, then the rest; the order is fixed on open and whenever the search changes (`usePickerOrder`), so a tick with the search unchanged never moves a row; a tick while the search has text clears the search (`EntityDropdownItems.onSearchClear`; the phone's one shared search) — the press keeps the focus in the search, a keyboard tick's row keeps the focus at its new place. With text typed in the desktop search (`SelectionPanelBody`), one row is highlighted (`.dropdownItemSearchPick`, the hover look; `data-search-pick`; the search's `aria-activedescendant` — rows carry `useId` ids): a named row always wins — the first one shown; the None row only when no named row is shown (`none` typed; `searchPickId`). The search is a `combobox` (`aria-autocomplete="list"`; `aria-expanded` / `aria-controls` the rows' listbox while it shows — `EntityDropdownItems` reports it through the context); ↓ goes to the highlighted row when there is one, else the rows' tab stop; while the focus is in the rows the highlight's tint is dropped (`[role='listbox']:focus-within`), so one row reads current. Enter / Tab during an IME composition pick nothing. Tab (no Shift) ticks it (never unticks it) and Enter toggles it, through the rows' own `pick` (registered over `SearchPickContext`, so the Is not / preview writes are the host's `onToggle` as for a click); the search clears and keeps the focus. With no text, Tab moves on; no row shown, nothing is picked. The picker bodies (`SelectionPanelBody`, `EntityDropdownItems` and `MarkerClausePicker`: Priority's condition as those two over the markers (Is / Is not, Select all / Deselect all, `No priority`, the rows in their fixed order); footnote `Urgent includes urgent by due date.`; the group is named `Priority`; `MarkersPanel.module.css`) live in `filters/FilterPanels.tsx`, shared with the list widgets' filter pickers; panels portal through `usePopoverAnchor` (flip + clamp) at `--z-dialog-nested` People / Status / Tags pickers (the Filter menu, the phone sheet) open with the Is / Is not toggle (`FilterPanels.FilterModeToggle`: a two-segment `radiogroup` `<Field>: is or is not`, arrow keys switch; touch height on the sheet). Both toggles are `FilterPanels.SegmentedToggle` (a segmented radio group: arrow keys move and wrap, an `accent` choice reads in the accent when picked, `inline` / `touch` / `disabled` variants). "Is" keeps the ticks-show model (no filter = all ticked, an inactive picker previews nothing ticked); "is not" ticks the hidden values (nothing ticked = no filter — the shared state holds an "is not" with nothing ticked until the first tick or the picker closes). Switching keeps the ticks. An "is not" row reads `not Waiting`; the phone section shows that value instead of a count; the People footer's Review goes to the people not ticked. |
| DateFilterPanel | components/shared/filters/DateFilterPanel.tsx, DateFilterPanel.module.css, date-filter-popover.ts | The date filters (`{ dates, initialField?, onChange, variant?: 'popover' \| 'sheet', autoFocus?, host? }`, over the `dates` map — one clause per date type, all must match). Six tabs in two rows of three (D22 board B) — Due · Next follow-up · Last follow-up / Completed · Created · Modified (`role=tablist` "Date type"; ← / → walk all six in reading order, ↑ / ↓ move between the rows in the same column, Home / End; roving tabindex; every label whole with its dot at 336px and in the 375px sheet — tight tab padding, 13px labels in the sheet, an ellipsis only as a fallback): the selected tab is the one being edited (local state: `initialField` — a chip's type —, else the first type that filters, else Due; selecting writes nothing); every type that filters is lit — accent + a 6px dot, named `<Type>, filtering`, `data-filtering`. Body: the type's title (`DATE_FIELD_LABELS`) + its clause in words or `Not filtering`; a Show segmented control on every type (`radiogroup` "Which tasks", cr25 P8 / D23 board B: **Calendar · Relative**, and on the optional dates — Due / Next follow-up / Last follow-up — **Any · None** too; arrows, Home / End move it): Any / None write `set` / `unset` and hide the range, which is kept in component state with its mode and comes back with Calendar / Relative (converted when the other is picked). Calendar / Relative is how the range's ends are entered, never stored: a range opens in the mode its ends read as (`entryModeOf`: every end a date → Calendar; any count or rolling day — a saved mix too — → Relative; a new range → Relative), a pick — or, without one, the mode shown when the type is first edited — is kept per type in component state, so an edit never flips it (emptying a Calendar range's date keeps Calendar and its date input; No end on a saved mix keeps Relative); Clear <Type> / Clear all forget it (a new range: Relative); switching converts the ends (`switchAnchorMode`: to Calendar each end's day today; to Relative each date as days from today, today as the `today` rolling day, a date more than 999 days away kept as a date) and keeps what it converted per end, so switching back while an end is unchanged restores it exactly (a rolling day, a count, a date); ends Calendar can't show are always Relative; the range's **Is / Is not** (schema v71, cr25 P4: a `Range` row with the id pickers' `FilterModeToggle`, `radiogroup` "<Type>: is or is not", touch-sized in the sheet; every type but Completed — `isNegatableDateField`; Is not picked before the range has an end is held in component state per type until an end is picked, as the id pickers keep a pending mode); From / To `DateAnchorInput`s in the range's mode (`label` `From` / `To`, `side` `from` / `to`, `day` from `useChipDay`, keyed by type; `zeroCount` — what a 0-day end counts, the last count written there — held here per type and end, so it outlives a tab or mode switch; a 132px date input + Set to…, or the number + its dropdown), under a Relative end its day today in small muted type (`resolvedEndWords`: `Mon 26 Oct and earlier` under a To with no From, `… and later` under a From with no To, else the day), the inputs' `aria-describedby`; "Also tasks with no due date" / "Also tasks with no next follow-up" / "Also tasks never followed up" (disabled until an end is set; a switch in the sheet) — under Is not a line in its place, "Tasks with no due date are kept." / "Tasks with no next follow-up are kept." / "Tasks never followed up are kept." (Created / Modified: none); Any / None explain lines per type ("Only tasks with no next follow-up.", "Only tasks never followed up."); the header words for them `any date` / `none` (Last follow-up `never`). Completed: a range only (Show Calendar · Relative, no Is not), with a line under it: "Shows tasks completed in this range, even where completed tasks are hidden.". `host.omit` leaves date types out (`DateFilterHost`; the check-in omits Completed): the tabs fill the rows in reading order, ↑ / ↓ from a column the shorter row lacks go to its last tab, and an omitted `initialField` opens on the first type offered. Footer: `Clear <Type>` (the selected type; disabled when off) and `Clear all` (`Clear all dates` in the sheet); neither closes. `data-shortcut-scope="none"`. Tokens `--color-seg-track` / `--color-seg-selected` / `--shadow-seg-selected`. `date-filter-popover`: the holder's class + its dialog name (`Date filters`) for callers that portal it An Is not chosen before the range has an end, which no clause can hold, is kept across closing the panel by `date-pending-not` (one memory per `PredicateChips` — `createPendingNotMemory`, outliving its popovers, given through `DatePendingNotContext` —; one for the screen filter, `screenPendingNot`). |
| PredicateChips, PredicateFieldPicker, PredicateFieldPickerBody, pickerLabel | components/shared/filters/PredicateChips.tsx, PredicateFieldPicker.tsx, picker-label.ts | A filter, **Graphical or an expression** (`TodoPredicate.expression`). **Graphical** (`GraphicalChips`): a `TodoPredicate` as chips, every chip ANDed (field name muted + value from `utils/filter-chips`; one chip per date type that filters, one per Priority condition; click opens that field's picker — a date chip the date panel on its type, the chip shown open (`aria-expanded`; D21's open trigger: the `--trigger-open-ring` accent ring, the 12 % `--color-trigger-open-fill`, its value in `--color-accent-text`, the focus ring layered outside — so + Filter while its menu is open) —, × clears it — a date chip only its type) plus "+ Filter" (a `MenuPopover`, `filter-menu.tsx` `ADD_FILTER_ITEMS`, in the top bar's order: Priority, Tags, People, Dates, Status, Text contains…). Priority repeats: + Filter adds a condition, the picker opens on a chip's (`markerIndex`, as a date chip's `dateField`); emptied, a later chip's picker closes. A chip that goes (× / Delete) hands the focus to the chip now in its place, else the last chip, else + Filter — never `<body>`. The picker is the top bar's picker body for one field (`FilterPanels`: entity checklist — starts unticked when adding —, the Priority picker (`MarkerClausePicker`: Is / Is not over Focus / Urgent / Important / No priority; several values are OR; stays open for several picks; with no search box, opening it focuses its rows), or a text box (`Text contains`, × = `Clear text filter`); or `DateFilterPanel` in a `Date filters` dialog, opened on the chip's type or, from + Filter › Dates, the first offered type the filter doesn't filter yet — so it adds a chip — (`freeDateField` over the host's `offeredDateFields`; every type filtering, the panel's default: the first that filters), portaled at a point. People / tag rows (`FilterPanels.EntityDropdownItems` with `entityMenu`, also in the top bar's People / Tags pickers): a right-click opens the person's / tag's `PickerEntityMenu` without ticking the row (not on None; the right button's press keeps the focus in the search; Show tasks closes the picker — `onShowTasks`); the rows are a multi-select `listbox` (named after the field) with one tab stop: ↓ from the search goes in, ↑ on the first row goes back, ↑ / ↓ / Home / End move, Space / Enter tick, the context-menu key opens the menu under the row; their keys reach no app shortcut (`data-shortcut-scope="none"`). **Two layouts** (`layout`): `line` (default; the list's filter line — one row) and `stack` (the editors: Edit list popover and phone sheet, the check-in's Which tasks), a column (`data-testid="filter-stack"`) — one chip per row, the + button on the last, reading **+ Another filter (AND)** once a chip is there (`+ Filter` with none, and always on the line) — where every chip is a disclosure (a chevron after its ×; a click anywhere but the × toggles). **A chip is two sibling controls** (`ListFilterChip`, cr31 F9): its own button (label + value, `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded`; Enter / Space open, Delete / Backspace remove; a press on the plate around it is its) and its × (`Remove filter`); the wrapper (`data-testid="list-filter-chip"`) is no control, and the keyboard focus rings the whole chip. In both, a chip's picker opens as a **popover** under the chip (`list-filter-picker`, `FieldPopover`, portaled, so an editor never reflows or scrolls for it); one at a time; a second click on the open chip closes it (its press is the popover's outside click — `pressedOpen` keeps the click from reopening it); Esc closes it (consumed in a dialog, so the editor stays). **The chips keep the order they were added in** (`utils/filter-row-order`: typed rows in component state, from the stored `FILTER_FIELD_ORDER` order at mount; cr31 A6): a filter added from the + button goes last whatever its field, a removed chip's row goes (added again, it goes last; a removed Priority chip's later conditions move down); a predicate replaced from outside sorts its new chips last in the stored order. `PredicateFieldPicker` / `PredicateFieldPickerBody` edit any `FilterClauses` through one `onChange`. **The editors' mode switch** (`stack` only): above the block, **Graphical \| Expression** (a `radiogroup` `Filter mode`; an ⓘ `About filter modes` whose tooltip says what each is). Expression turns the chips into the expression that filters the same (`utils/predicate-expression.asExpression`, written only when the filter narrows by something) and shows `ExpressionField` (focused when switched to by a click, and by Edit expression — Clear and use Graphical focuses + Filter, cr33 U6; `allowPeople` off when `omitFields` holds People, `allowCompleted` when `dateHost.omit` holds Completed — the check-in —, `allowDone` from the prop — to be off for a pane and the check-in, cr33 F9); past `EXPRESSION_NODES_MAX` / `EXPRESSION_TEXT_MAX` the switch refuses, staying Graphical with a line saying so (`aria-live`, cr33 M1); the switch is one tab stop, ←/→ move the choice and the focus (cr33 U9); Graphical turns an expression back into chips when every top-level term is one (`asGraphical`), else shows the **locked panel** (`data-testid="expression-locked"`: `This filter is an expression`, its text, **Edit expression** · **Clear and use Graphical** — `withExpression(p, null)`). The mode follows the filter: one that becomes an expression elsewhere (undo, a reload) shows in Expression, one put back to chips elsewhere (Revert, undo) in Graphical; emptied, it stays where it is. **On the line** an expression is **read back in words** (`ExpressionWords`) — a button opening Edit list when the host passes `onOpenExpression` (tooltip the exact text, then `Click to edit`), else a focusable span; no + Filter beside it. Used by the list widget's filter line, the list editor form and the check-in dialog's Which tasks (`addLabel` names the + Filter menu; `inDialog` makes a picker's Esc close only the picker — `PredicateFieldPicker.consumeEscape`; `omitFields` leaves fields out of + Filter — the check-in's People; `dateHost` goes to every date panel it opens — the check-in leaves Completed out). The picker popovers' surface is `FilterPicker.module.css .dropdownPanel` — the popover tokens (D21; never darker than what it opens over, hover rows `--color-tint-8`), level 2 from an editor or a dialog. Styles: `ListFilterChips.module.css` — the `.chip` base and every chip composed from it in the one file (also the list widget's screen-filter chip and group · sort summary), the mode switch and the locked panel; 32px tall, 8px radius under `[data-touch-chips]` (the list widget sets it on a phone), so the filter line and the list editor form look the same. Entity pickers have the same Is / Is not toggle (the picker holds an "is not" with nothing ticked; ids and switch written together through `withIdFilter`); an "is not" chip (`data-negated`) reads `not …` and shows the app tooltip saying what it hides. Its popovers anchor to the chip's (or + Filter's) rect — `pointBelow` keeps it in the `PopoverPoint` (`rect`), `anchorAtPoint` turns it into a `rect` anchor — and draw the caret at it (D21; gap `CARET_GAP_PX`, a flip above goes above the chip's top). |

| DragOverlayTask, createTaskDropAnimation, TaskDropLandingContext, TaskDropLanding | components/workspace/DragOverlayTask.tsx (+ `.module.css`), components/workspace/task-drop-animation.ts, components/task/dnd/task-drop-landing.ts | The lifted task row (D25 E4): a ghost `TaskRow` — and its optional `label`, under the row: the Planner's `PlannerDragLabel` — in `.overlay` (`[data-drag-overlay="task"]`; the surface-high fill, `--shadow-drag-overlay` plus a 1px inset accent ring, no padding or border, so it sits exactly on the row it was picked up from; no opacity, no offset). **On grab** a CSS animation (`--drag-lift-duration`, 160 ms, `--spring-easing`) takes it from the row's resting look (no transform, no shadow) to `scale(1.02)` with the shadow (no tilt), on the inner element (dnd-kit owns the wrapper's transform). **On drop** `createTaskDropAnimation()` (a closure: `animation` for `DragOverlay`'s `dropAnimation`, `land(landing)` for the drop handlers; a drag's start resets it to `settle`) settles it from its computed pose (shrinking back to rest) (a drop mid-lift too) over `--drag-settle-duration` (200 ms) on the spring curve, Web Animations both: `settle` — the wrapper moves onto the dragged row (its new spot after a manual reorder, which the list writes at once; else its origin: Esc, a drop on nothing, a no-op) measured from the wrapper's own box (dnd-kit's measure takes the scaled row's, wider and taller), its fill fading in the second half, the row hidden (`opacity: 0`) until it lands; `in-place` — `land` copies the overlay beside itself at once and fades the copy (opacity and scale, no move), since dnd-kit animates nothing once the dragged row has left (a row moved to another group renders under a new drag id) and the overlay goes. dnd-kit's drop clone reuses the same keyed DOM, so the computed pose is the one on screen. The settling overlay takes no pointer events. **Reduced motion**: the `.overlay` stays untransformed (its own `prefers-reduced-motion` rule — the global one only shortens the animation, which would end scaled) and the drop goes at once. `e2e/lifted-drag.spec.ts` |

## Layout

| Abstraction | Location | Purpose |
|-------------|----------|---------|

also while the panel is open, with no filter — neutral then), then `StoragePill`, the Settings gear (opens the modal; pressed tint while open) and, last at the right, **New task** (solid accent, the + alone — name and keys in its tooltip / `New task` label; opens QuickAddBar; in the right zone, so the fold counts it in the base). No theme toggle. Short of room (`useTopBarFold`, measured) the field narrows (to 240px), then the layout tabs fold into the picker, then the field narrows further (to 160px); the filters never fold — they are the field's pills, which fade with `+N more`. Past `MAX_LAYOUT_TABS` (30) layouts the picker shows from the start (the tabs aren't measured). **The filtered-tasks panel** (`FilteredTasksPanel`, while `ui-store.filterPanelOpen`) hangs from the tray, which is its filter editor: the field's search results are off (the panel's rows are the results; typing narrows them), the field's Esc puts its typed text back first (and stays), a press on the bar outside the tray (`onMouseDownCapture`, in the page — not in a layer portaled out) closes the panel before doing its own thing. **The panel never changes the bar's layout** (the fold doesn't know it): the field and the Filter menu edit the filter over it (their pickers open above the panel; Clear all filters leaves it open on every open task), and the tray and the panel are one Tab cycle (`tabAcross`: the funnel, the field, the Filter button, the counter, then the panel). The Filter menu draws its caret at its button, which takes the open trigger's accent ring and text while open (`[aria-expanded='true']`, D21). While the layout edit mode is on (on the workspace route) `TopBar` renders `LayoutEditBar` in place of the resting bar (`RestingTopBar`); the open layout's tab menu and the picker have **Edit layout…** first (no button in the bar for it). |

The hook (`useTopBarCentre(bar, spacer, tray, right)`) measures after each resize of the bar, the tray (and its parts) or the right zone, on the next frame; `start` takes out the gap's laid-out width (flex shrinks it first when the bar is short), so the result doesn't depend on it. ## UI Constants

| Abstraction | Location | Purpose |
|-------------|----------|---------|
| Design tokens + base rules | styles/tokens.css | Design tokens (dark / light themes via `[data-theme]`, tints, shadows, radii, z-index, spacing, type) and the global base rules (reset, focus ring, native `select` options on the opaque surface colour, the one `prefers-reduced-motion` rule: every animation / transition ends in 1 ms). The only place a rule forks per theme: components read tokens (**popovers** (D21): `--color-popover-bg` (dark `#2a2825`, light white), `--color-popover-ring` (a light hairline in dark, a border tone in light) inside `--shadow-dropdown` (dark: a top highlight, the ring, a 3px dark rim, contact / near / far layers; light: the ring, a contact and an ambient layer), each with a `-2` twin for level 2 that `[data-popover-level='2']` swaps in, and `--color-popover-text-muted`, the muted text inside every `[data-popover-level]` (AA on both lifted grounds); `--color-dialog-bg` for the dialogs (unchanged by D21); `--shadow-raised` for small raised things that aren't popovers (drag labels, the FAB, the snackbar, the recent-task pill); `--color-accent-text` for the accent as text, the AA `--color-text-muted`; the accent tints `--color-accent-bg-*` / `-glow` / `-focus` / `-shadow*` are `color-mix`es of `--color-accent`, so a custom accent tints with its own colour), pinned by `src/test/styles/` (contrast ratios, no `[data-theme]` selector in a component stylesheet, every token used and every `var()` defined) D19: **one focus ring** — `:focus-visible` in `@layer focus-ring` draws `--focus-ring` (2px of the surface, then 2px of `--focus-ring-color`: the layout's hue, else the accent) as a box-shadow that follows each element's radius; being layered, any component box-shadow wins, so an element with its own shadow adds the ring layers in its `:focus-visible` rule, one a scroller would clip (tabs, menu rows) uses `--focus-ring-inset`, a field that marks focus its own way sets `box-shadow: none`, and a dialog / list box holding focus (`tabindex=-1`) shows none. **Scroll-edge fades**: `[data-can-scroll-up]` / `[data-can-scroll-down]` (`utils/scroll-edges`) mask 18px / 22px, from `--scroll-fade-inset` under a sticky header. The layout hues `--hue-<name>` (six, light twins at 3:1), `--cell-header-shade`, `--grain-display` (dark only), `--cal-late-due` / `--cal-late-followup` (a late heat-map day's number, 4.5:1 on every step), `--spring-duration` / `--spring-easing` (`::view-transition-*`, none under reduced motion), `--drag-lift-duration` / `--drag-settle-duration` (the lifted task row's grab and drop, `DragOverlayTask`), `--row-title-start` (where a desktop task row's title starts). The lists (D25): `--list-group-gap` (18px above a group) and the group header's height and gap at Lines (`--list-group-header-height-lines`, `--list-group-gap-lines`), `--font-size-group-name` / `--font-size-row-meta` (11.5px cells) / `--font-size-task-line` (a Lines title), `--font-weight-regular` (a row's title, 400; flair P5) / `--font-weight-medium` (a Lines title, 500), `--task-row-stacked-height` / `--task-line-height`, the priority ring's fills `--color-marker-urgent-fill` / `--color-marker-important-fill`, and the Lines boxes' fills `--color-deadline-soon` (the deadline at 45 %), `--color-row-tone-plain` / `--color-row-tone-stale` (the muted text at 80 % / 35 %; light twins; pinned by `contrast.test.ts`). The light theme is warm paper (ground `#f7f6f3`, surface `#fcfbf9`, surface-high and header `#f3f1ed`, shadows and borders toward the paper, cards with a white top highlight, text `#1f1d1a`, accent `#2f9a8f`). The open trigger (D21): `--trigger-open-ring` (`0 0 0 1px` accent) and `--color-trigger-open-fill` (12 % accent), with `--color-accent-text`; a focused open trigger layers `var(--trigger-open-ring), var(--focus-ring)`. `--color-popover-rim` / `-2` (the dark shadow's 3px rim as a colour, transparent in light; swapped at level 2) edges the carets and Edit list's arrow. Under `[data-popover-level]` `--focus-ring` is declared again with the popover's ground as its gap. **Type** (UX cleanup 5A): every `font-size` in a stylesheet is a `--font-size-*` token — the scale `xs` 11 · `sm` 12 · `md` 13 · `body` 14 · `lg` 16 · `xl` 22 (with its same-sized names `meta` 12, `task` / `nav` 14), `input` (the phone's 16px no-zoom field text), the `display-*` ladder of figures on widget faces (`3xs` 15 a goal's target … `md` 22 a face's main count … `2xl` 40), and role tokens for what isn't text on the scale: `avatar` / `avatar-md` / `avatar-lg` (8 / 9 / 10px initials sized to their circle), `badge` (10px, a count in a 14–16px round badge) and `badge-capsule` (10.5px, a list tab's due capsule), `glyph-xs` / `glyph-sm` / `glyph-lg` (8 / 10 / 18px: a sort mark; a caret, a ✓, the review rail's clock; a dialog's ×), `donut-total` (a Breakdown donut's figure in its viewBox), `row-meta`, `task-line`, `group-name`, `title`; no literal, in CSS or an inline `fontSize`, and no `--font-size-legacy-*` (`test/styles/font-sizes.test.ts`). Form controls inherit their font size and family (the browser's 13.33px default is reset in the base rules). The small × buttons — a tab's, a filter chip's (top bar and editors), Quick Add's chips' — and the desktop row's checkbox take presses over 24 × 24px through an `::after` round their drawn size; the priority gutter, a date's clear × and Lines rows stay small (J20; `e2e/hit-targets.spec.ts`) |
| DEFAULT_ENTITY_COLOR | constants.ts | Default color '#537FE7' for new tags and statuses (incl. NLP-created `#tag` rows) |
| NEUTRAL_PERSON_COLOR | constants.ts | Neutral grey '#9CA3AF' used for people with no `color` (PeopleEditor swatch + AvatarStack fill + people group-header swatch) |
